          desc: "家具、布艺、灯具、地毯、装饰画、摆件搭配。",
          variants: [
            { key: "variant_a", field: "variant_a_prompt", label: "方案 A", name: "克制高级", file: "10_室内设计_软装搭配方案_01_克制高级.png" },
            { key: "variant_b", field: "variant_b_prompt", label: "方案 B", name: "温暖生活", file: "10_室内设计_软装搭配方案_02_温暖生活.png" },
            { key: "variant_c", field: "variant_c_prompt", label: "方案 C", name: "设计感强化", file: "10_室内设计_软装搭配方案_03_设计感强化.png" }
          ]
        },
        material_replacement: {
          title: "室内｜墙面 / 地面材质替换",
          designer_type: "interior_design",
          icon: "替",
          desc: "墙面、地面、石材、木饰面、微水泥材质替换。",
          variants: [
            { key: "variant_a", field: "variant_a_prompt", label: "方案 A", name: "墙面材质替换", file: "11_室内设计_墙面地面材质替换_01_墙面材质替换.png" },
            { key: "variant_b", field: "variant_b_prompt", label: "方案 B", name: "地面材质替换", file: "11_室内设计_墙面地面材质替换_02_地面材质替换.png" },
            { key: "variant_c", field: "variant_c_prompt", label: "方案 C", name: "墙地整体协调", file: "11_室内设计_墙面地面材质替换_03_墙地整体协调.png" }
          ]
        },
        lighting_mood: {
          title: "室内｜灯光氛围调整",
          designer_type: "interior_design",
          icon: "光",
          desc: "自然光、间接光、重点照明、夜间高级氛围。",
          variants: [
            { key: "variant_a", field: "variant_a_prompt", label: "方案 A", name: "白天自然光", file: "12_室内设计_灯光氛围调整_01_白天自然光.png" },
            { key: "variant_b", field: "variant_b_prompt", label: "方案 B", name: "傍晚暖光", file: "12_室内设计_灯光氛围调整_02_傍晚暖光.png" },
            { key: "variant_c", field: "variant_c_prompt", label: "方案 C", name: "夜间高级氛围", file: "12_室内设计_灯光氛围调整_03_夜间高级氛围.png" }
          ]
        }
      };

      const PROMPT_FIELDS = [
        ["image_roles", "参考图说明"],
        ["global_prompt", "全局任务"],
        ["variant_a_prompt", "方案 A"],
        ["variant_b_prompt", "方案 B"],
        ["variant_c_prompt", "方案 C"],
        ["consistency_prompt", "一致性"],
        ["negative_prompt", "反向约束"]
      ];

      const DEFAULT_MODE_OPTIONS = Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(MODE_DEFS).map(([key, def]) => [def.title, key])
      );

      const DEFAULT_PROMPTS = Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(MODE_DEFS).map(([key, def]) => [key, createDefaultPrompt(def)])
      );

      const $ = (selector) => document.querySelector(selector);
      const $$ = (selector) => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(selector));

      const appState = {
        pluginVersion: DEFAULT_PLUGIN_VERSION,
        designer: "graphic_design",
        skinKey: "winter_gray",
        previewMode: "template",
        selectedVariantKey: "variant_a",
        dirty: false,
        runtime: {},
        autoRetry: true,
        lastModalText: "",
        lastModalImageUrl: "",
        lastModalRetryPayload: null,
        modeModalType: "add",
        saveTimer: null,
        retryStamp: {}
      };

      let modeOptions = { ...DEFAULT_MODE_OPTIONS };
      let modeMeta = deepClone(MODE_DEFS);
      let configByMode = deepClone(DEFAULT_PROMPTS);
      let currentModeKey = "brand_key_visual";
      let currentField = "image_roles";

      function deepClone(obj) {
        return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(obj || {}));
      }

      function escapeHtml(value) {
        return String(value ?? "")
          .replaceAll("&", "&amp;")
          .replaceAll("<", "&lt;")
          .replaceAll(">", "&gt;")
          .replaceAll('"', "&quot;")
          .replaceAll("'", "&#039;");
      }

      function createDefaultPrompt(def) {
        return {
          designer_type: def.designer_type || "graphic_design",
          preview_urls: {},
          image_roles: "Image 1 = 主体素材；Image 2 = 风格参考；Image 3 = 配色参考；Image 4 = 版式参考；Image 5 = 材质或纹理参考；Image 6 = 背景或场景参考。",
          global_prompt: `任务：${cleanTitle(def.title)}。请基于上传参考图生成高审美设计图，保持构图高级、层级清晰、材质自然、画面干净。不要直接生成文字，不要乱码，不要水印。`,
          variant_a_prompt: `${def.variants?.[0]?.label || "方案 A"}：${def.variants?.[0]?.name || "稳定高级版"}。构图稳定、清晰、适合正式商业使用。`,
          variant_b_prompt: `${def.variants?.[1]?.label || "方案 B"}：${def.variants?.[1]?.name || "视觉强化版"}。增强视觉吸引力、层次、空间与光影。`,
          variant_c_prompt: `${def.variants?.[2]?.label || "方案 C"}：${def.variants?.[2]?.name || "实验设计版"}。更有设计张力，但仍保持高级可落地。`,
          consistency_prompt: "三张图必须保持同一设计系统、同一视觉气质、同一素材逻辑，只允许方案表达差异。",
          negative_prompt: "不要真实文字，不要乱码字体，不要水印，不要二维码，不要价格，不要促销标签，不要低清晰度，不要明显 AI 扭曲，不要畸形结构，不要杂乱拼贴，不要廉价滤镜。"
        };
      }

      function cleanTitle(title) {
        return String(title || "").replace(/^平面｜|^室内｜/, "").trim();
      }

      function toast(message) {
        const el = $("#toast");
        el.textContent = message;
        el.classList.add("show");
        clearTimeout(window.__designerToastTimer);
        window.__designerToastTimer = setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove("show"), 2400);
      }

      function setStatus(message) {
        $("#statusBox").textContent = message;
      }

      function setDirty(value) {
        appState.dirty = value;
        $("#saveBtn").classList.toggle("dirty", value);
        $("#saveBtn").textContent = value ? "保存配置 *" : "保存配置";
        $("#saveHint").textContent = value ? "未保存" : "已保存";
      }

      function saveUiState() {
        localStorage.setItem(UI_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify({
          designer: appState.designer,
          skinKey: appState.skinKey,
          previewMode: appState.previewMode,
          selectedVariantKey: appState.selectedVariantKey,
          currentModeKey,
          currentField,
          autoRetry: appState.autoRetry
        }));
      }

      function loadUiState() {
        try {
          const raw = localStorage.getItem(UI_STATE_KEY);
          if (!raw) return;
          const data = JSON.parse(raw);
          if (data.designer) appState.designer = data.designer;
          if (data.skinKey) appState.skinKey = data.skinKey;
          if (data.previewMode) appState.previewMode = data.previewMode;
          if (data.selectedVariantKey) appState.selectedVariantKey = data.selectedVariantKey;
          if (data.currentModeKey) currentModeKey = data.currentModeKey;
          if (data.currentField) currentField = data.currentField;
          if (typeof data.autoRetry === "boolean") appState.autoRetry = data.autoRetry;
        } catch {}
      }

      function normalizeSkinKey(value) {
        const raw = String(value || "");
        if (raw === "warm_yellow" || raw === "暖砂淡黄") return "warm_yellow";
        return "winter_gray";
      }

      function renderSkin() {
        document.body.dataset.skin = normalizeSkinKey(appState.skinKey);
        $$(".skin-toggle button").forEach((btn) => {
          btn.classList.toggle("active", btn.dataset.skin === appState.skinKey);
        });
        $("#infoSkin").textContent = appState.skinKey === "warm_yellow" ? "暖砂淡黄" : "冬之韵淡灰";
      }

      function titleByModeKey(key) {
        const found = Object.entries(modeOptions).find(([, v]) => v === key);
        return found?.[0] || modeMeta[key]?.title || key;
      }

      function modeDef(key) {
        const def = modeMeta[key] || MODE_DEFS[key] || {};
        return {
          title: titleByModeKey(key),
          designer_type: def.designer_type || configByMode[key]?.designer_type || "graphic_design",
          icon: def.icon || (titleByModeKey(key).replace(/^平面｜|^室内｜/, "").slice(0, 1) || "设"),
          desc: def.desc || "自定义设计模板。",
          variants: def.variants || createFallbackVariants(key)
        };
      }

      function createFallbackVariants(key) {
        return [
          { key: "variant_a", field: "variant_a_prompt", label: "方案 A", name: "稳定高级版", file: `${key}_variant_a.png` },
          { key: "variant_b", field: "variant_b_prompt", label: "方案 B", name: "视觉强化版", file: `${key}_variant_b.png` },
          { key: "variant_c", field: "variant_c_prompt", label: "方案 C", name: "实验设计版", file: `${key}_variant_c.png` }
        ];
      }

      function ensureModeExists(key, title = "") {
        if (!key) return;
        if (!Object.values(modeOptions).includes(key)) {
          modeOptions[title || modeMeta[key]?.title || key] = key;
        }
        if (!modeMeta[key]) {
          modeMeta[key] = {
            title: title || key,
            designer_type: appState.designer,
            icon: (title || key).slice(0, 1),
            desc: "自定义设计模板。",
            variants: createFallbackVariants(key)
          };
        }
        if (!configByMode[key]) {
          configByMode[key] = createDefaultPrompt(modeDef(key));
        }
        if (!configByMode[key].preview_urls || typeof configByMode[key].preview_urls !== "object") {
          configByMode[key].preview_urls = {};
        }
        configByMode[key].designer_type = configByMode[key].designer_type || modeDef(key).designer_type;
      }

      function restoreBuiltInModes() {
        Object.entries(DEFAULT_MODE_OPTIONS).forEach(([display, key]) => {
          if (!Object.values(modeOptions).includes(key)) {
            modeOptions[display] = key;
          }
        });

        Object.entries(MODE_DEFS).forEach(([key, def]) => {
          modeMeta[key] = {
            ...def,
            ...(modeMeta[key] || {}),
            title: (modeMeta[key] && modeMeta[key].title) || def.title,
            designer_type: def.designer_type,
            icon: (modeMeta[key] && modeMeta[key].icon) || def.icon,
            desc: (modeMeta[key] && modeMeta[key].desc) || def.desc,
            variants: (modeMeta[key] && modeMeta[key].variants) || def.variants
          };

          if (!configByMode[key]) {
            configByMode[key] = createDefaultPrompt(modeMeta[key]);
          }

          configByMode[key].designer_type = def.designer_type;
          if (!configByMode[key].preview_urls || typeof configByMode[key].preview_urls !== "object") {
            configByMode[key].preview_urls = {};
          }
        });
      }

      function getCurrentModeData() {
        ensureModeExists(currentModeKey);
        return configByMode[currentModeKey];
      }

      function getVariants(key = currentModeKey) {
        return modeDef(key).variants || createFallbackVariants(key);
      }

      function getCurrentVariant() {
        const variants = getVariants();
        return variants.find((item) => item.key === appState.selectedVariantKey) || variants[0];
      }

      function snapshotCurrentText() {
        const textarea = $("#promptText");
        if (!textarea) return;
        ensureModeExists(currentModeKey);
        configByMode[currentModeKey][currentField] = textarea.value;
      }

      function cacheConfig() {
        try {
          localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(buildExportConfig({ skipSnapshot: true })));
        } catch {}
      }

      async function fetchJsonFirst(routes, options = {}) {
        let lastError = null;
        for (const route of routes) {
          try {
            const resp = await fetch(route, {
              cache: "no-store",
              headers: options.body ? { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...(options.headers || {}) } : options.headers,
              ...options
            });
            if (!resp.ok) throw new Error(`${route} HTTP ${resp.status}`);
            const json = await resp.json().catch(() => ({}));
            return { route, json };
          } catch (error) {
            lastError = error;
          }
        }
        throw lastError || new Error("所有接口不可用");
      }

      async function loadConfig() {
        loadUiState();
        renderSkin();
        setStatus("配置状态：正在读取远端配置...");

        try {
          const { route, json } = await fetchJsonFirst(CONFIG_GET_ROUTES);
          normalizeLoadedConfig(json);
          setStatus(`配置状态：已读取 ${route}`);
        } catch {
          const cached = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) || localStorage.getItem(LIVE_CONFIG_KEY);
          if (cached) {
            try {
              const parsed = JSON.parse(cached);
              normalizeLoadedConfig(parsed.config || parsed.prompt_config || parsed);
              setStatus("配置状态：远端不可用，已读取本地缓存");
            } catch {
              normalizeLoadedConfig({});
              setStatus("配置状态：使用页面默认配置");
            }
          } else {
            normalizeLoadedConfig({});
            setStatus("配置状态：使用页面默认配置");
          }
        }

        restoreBuiltInModes();
        ensureCurrentModeValid();
        renderAll();
        setDirty(false);
        publishSync("load_config", false);
      }

      function normalizeLoadedConfig(payload) {
        const config = payload.prompt_config || payload.config || payload.designer_template?.prompt_config || payload;
        if (!config || typeof config !== "object") return;

        appState.pluginVersion = config.plugin_version || payload.plugin_version || DEFAULT_PLUGIN_VERSION;

        if (config.theme_skins && typeof config.theme_skins === "object") {
          Object.assign(THEME_SKINS, config.theme_skins);
        }

        if (config.mode_options && typeof config.mode_options === "object") {
          // 重要：不要用远端/本地配置完全覆盖内置模板。
          // 用户旧配置里可能只有平面设计或只有一个模板；这里必须合并，避免“室内设计”全部消失。
          const mergedOptions = { ...DEFAULT_MODE_OPTIONS };
          Object.entries(config.mode_options).forEach(([display, key]) => {
            const k = String(key || "").trim();
            const d = String(display || k).trim();
            if (!k || !d) return;
            mergedOptions[d] = k;
            if (!modeMeta[k]) {
              modeMeta[k] = {
                title: d,
                designer_type: d.startsWith("室内") ? "interior_design" : "graphic_design",
                icon: cleanTitle(d).slice(0, 1) || "设",
                desc: "自定义设计模板。",
                variants: createFallbackVariants(k)
              };
            } else {
              modeMeta[k].title = d;
            }
          });
          modeOptions = mergedOptions;
        }

        if (config.mode_meta && typeof config.mode_meta === "object") {
          Object.entries(config.mode_meta).forEach(([key, meta]) => {
            modeMeta[key] = { ...(modeMeta[key] || {}), ...(meta || {}) };
          });
        }

        const topPreview = config.preview_urls && typeof config.preview_urls === "object" ? config.preview_urls : {};
        const modes = config.modes && typeof config.modes === "object" ? config.modes : {};

        Object.entries(modes).forEach(([key, data]) => {
          ensureModeExists(key, titleByModeKey(key));
          configByMode[key] = {
            ...createDefaultPrompt(modeDef(key)),
            ...(data || {}),
            preview_urls: {
              ...(topPreview[key] || {}),
              ...((data || {}).preview_urls || {})
            }
          };
        });

        Object.values(modeOptions).forEach((key) => ensureModeExists(key, titleByModeKey(key)));
        restoreBuiltInModes();
      }

      function ensureCurrentModeValid() {
        const keys = Object.values(modeOptions).filter((key) => modeDef(key).designer_type === appState.designer);
        if (!keys.includes(currentModeKey)) {
          currentModeKey = keys[0] || Object.values(modeOptions)[0] || "brand_key_visual";
        }
        ensureModeExists(currentModeKey);
        const variants = getVariants();
        if (!variants.some((item) => item.key === appState.selectedVariantKey)) {
          appState.selectedVariantKey = variants[0]?.key || "variant_a";
        }
        if (!PROMPT_FIELDS.some(([key]) => key === currentField)) {
          currentField = "image_roles";
        }
      }

      function renderDesignerSwitch() {
        $$(".designer-btn").forEach((btn) => {
          btn.classList.toggle("active", btn.dataset.designer === appState.designer);
        });
        $("#infoDesigner").textContent = appState.designer === "interior_design" ? "室内设计" : "平面设计";
      }

      function renderModes() {
        const list = $("#modeList");
        list.innerHTML = "";

        let entries = Object.entries(modeOptions)
          .filter(([, key]) => modeDef(key).designer_type === appState.designer);

        if (!entries.length) {
          restoreBuiltInModes();
          entries = Object.entries(modeOptions)
            .filter(([, key]) => modeDef(key).designer_type === appState.designer);
        }

        entries.forEach(([title, key]) => {
          ensureModeExists(key, title);
          const def = modeDef(key);
          const btn = document.createElement("button");
          btn.type = "button";
          btn.className = `mode-card ${key === currentModeKey ? "active" : ""}`;
          btn.innerHTML = `
            <div class="mode-sign">${escapeHtml(def.icon)}</div>
            <div class="mode-text">
              <strong>${escapeHtml(cleanTitle(title))}</strong>
              <span>${escapeHtml(def.desc)}</span>
            </div>
          `;
          btn.onclick = () => {
            if (key === currentModeKey) return;
            snapshotCurrentText();
            currentModeKey = key;
            currentField = "image_roles";
            appState.selectedVariantKey = getVariants(key)[0]?.key || "variant_a";
            setDirty(true);
            saveUiState();
            renderAll();
            publishSync("switch_mode", true);
          };
          list.appendChild(btn);
        });
      }

      function renderPromptTabs() {
        const box = $("#promptTabs");
        box.innerHTML = "";
        PROMPT_FIELDS.forEach(([key, label]) => {
          const btn = document.createElement("button");
          btn.className = `prompt-tab ${key === currentField ? "active" : ""}`;
          btn.textContent = label;
          btn.onclick = () => {
            snapshotCurrentText();
            currentField = key;
            saveUiState();
            renderPromptEditor();
            publishSync("switch_field", false);
          };
          box.appendChild(btn);
        });
      }

      function renderPromptEditor() {
        const data = getCurrentModeData();
        const fieldDef = PROMPT_FIELDS.find(([key]) => key === currentField) || PROMPT_FIELDS[0];
        $("#fieldTitle").textContent = fieldDef[1];
        $("#fieldName").textContent = fieldDef[0];
        $("#promptText").value = data[fieldDef[0]] || "";
        $("#charCount").textContent = `${$("#promptText").value.length} 字符`;
        $("#currentTemplateLabel").textContent = `当前模板：${cleanTitle(titleByModeKey(currentModeKey))}`;
        $("#currentTemplateDesc").textContent = modeDef(currentModeKey).desc;
        $$(".prompt-tab").forEach((btn) => btn.classList.toggle("active", btn.textContent === fieldDef[1]));
      }

      function assetUrl(file) {
        if (!file) return "";
        if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(file) || /^data:/i.test(file)) return file;
        return ASSET_BASE + encodeURIComponent(file);
      }

      function getUploadedPreviewUrl(variantKey) {
        const urls = getCurrentModeData().preview_urls || {};
        return urls[variantKey] || "";
      }

      function getLatestRuntimeGroup() {
        const latest = appState.runtime.latest || appState.runtime;
        const groups =
          latest.groups ||
          latest.results_groups ||
          appState.runtime.groups ||
          appState.runtime.results ||
          [];
        if (Array.isArray(groups) && groups.length) {
          return groups.find((group) => groupMatchesMode(group)) || groups[0];
        }
        if (latest.views || latest.results) return latest;
        return null;
      }

      function groupMatchesMode(group) {
        const mode = String(group?.mode_actual || group?.mode_key || group?.template_key || group?.template_display || group?.mode_display || "");
        return !mode || mode === currentModeKey || mode === titleByModeKey(currentModeKey) || mode === cleanTitle(titleByModeKey(currentModeKey));
      }

      function getRuntimeItem(variantKey) {
        const group = getLatestRuntimeGroup();
        if (!group || !groupMatchesMode(group)) return null;
        const views = group.views || group.results || group.outputs || {};
        return views[variantKey] || views[variantKey.replace("variant_", "方案")] || null;
      }

      function pickImage(item) {
        if (!item) return "";
        if (typeof item === "string") return item;
        return item.image || item.url || item.image_url || item.imageUrl || item.oss_url || item.ossUrl || item.public_url || item.publicUrl || item.preview_url || item.previewUrl || "";
      }

      function normalizeRuntimeItem(item) {
        if (!item) return { status: "missing", error: "", image: "" };
        if (typeof item === "string") return { status: "success", image: item, error: "" };
        const image = pickImage(item);
        const error = item.error || item.message || item.reason || "";
        return {
          ...item,
          image,
          error,
          status: item.status || item.state || (error ? "failed" : image ? "success" : "missing")
        };
      }

      function previewSourceForVariant(variant) {
        const live = normalizeRuntimeItem(getRuntimeItem(variant.key));
        const liveImage = pickImage(live);
        if (liveImage) {
          return { url: liveImage, source: "实时结果", item: live, runtime: true };
        }

        const uploaded = getUploadedPreviewUrl(variant.key);
        if (uploaded) {
          return { url: uploaded, source: "OSS预览", item: { status: "success", image: uploaded, from_oss_preview: true }, runtime: false };
        }

        return {
          url: assetUrl(variant.file),
          source: "模板预览",
          item: { status: "success", image: assetUrl(variant.file), from_template: true },
          runtime: false
        };
      }

      function renderTemplatePreview() {
        const variants = getVariants();
        const selected = getCurrentVariant();
        const selectedSource = previewSourceForVariant(selected);

        $("#previewBadge").textContent = `${selected.label} · ${selected.name}`;
        $("#previewMainImage").src = selectedSource.url;
        $("#infoVariant").textContent = `${selected.label} · ${selected.name}`;
        $("#infoMode").textContent = cleanTitle(titleByModeKey(currentModeKey));

        $("#thumbRow").innerHTML = variants.map((item) => {
          const src = previewSourceForVariant(item);
          return `
            <button class="thumb-card ${item.key === appState.selectedVariantKey ? "active" : ""}" data-variant="${escapeHtml(item.key)}">
              <img src="${escapeHtml(src.url)}" alt="${escapeHtml(item.label)} ${escapeHtml(item.name)}" loading="lazy" />
              <span>${escapeHtml(item.label)} · ${escapeHtml(item.name)}</span>
            </button>
          `;
        }).join("");

        $$("#thumbRow .thumb-card").forEach((btn) => {
          btn.onclick = () => {
            appState.selectedVariantKey = btn.dataset.variant;
            saveUiState();
            renderTemplatePreview();
            publishSync("switch_variant", false);
          };
        });
      }

      function renderRuntime() {
        const box = $("#runtimeGrid");
        const latest = appState.runtime.latest || appState.runtime;
        const groupsRaw = latest.groups || latest.results_groups || appState.runtime.groups || [];
        let groups = Array.isArray(groupsRaw) && groupsRaw.length ? groupsRaw : (latest.views || latest.results ? [latest] : []);
        groups = groups.filter((group) => groupMatchesMode(group));

        if (appState.previewMode === "failed") {
          groups = groups.filter((group) => getVariants().some((variant) => {
            const item = normalizeRuntimeItem((group.views || group.results || {})[variant.key]);
            const s = String(item.status || "").toLowerCase();
            return item.error || ["failed", "fail", "error", "missing"].includes(s);
          }));
        }

        if (!groups.length) {
          box.innerHTML = `<div class="placeholder">${appState.previewMode === "failed" ? "暂无报错结果" : "暂无最近生成结果"}<br>点击刷新结果读取节点运行结果。</div>`;
          return;
        }

        box.innerHTML = groups.map((group, groupIndex) => {
          const title = group.template_display || group.mode_display || titleByModeKey(currentModeKey);
          const sub = [
            group.output_strategy || "",
            group.model || "",
            group.updated_at ? new Date((Number(group.updated_at) > 10000000000 ? Number(group.updated_at) : Number(group.updated_at) * 1000)).toLocaleString() : ""
          ].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");

          const views = getVariants().map((variant) => {
            const item = normalizeRuntimeItem((group.views || group.results || {})[variant.key]);
            const image = pickImage(item);
            const status = String(item.status || "missing").toLowerCase();
            const failed = item.error || ["failed", "fail", "error"].includes(status);
            return `
              <div class="runtime-view ${failed ? "failed" : "success"}" data-group="${groupIndex}" data-variant="${escapeHtml(variant.key)}">
                ${
                  image
                    ? `<img src="${escapeHtml(image)}" alt="${escapeHtml(variant.label)}" loading="lazy" />`
                    : `<div class="placeholder" style="min-height:150px;border:0;border-radius:18px;">${failed ? "生成失败" : "暂无图片"}</div>`
                }
                <span>${escapeHtml(variant.label)} · ${failed ? "可重抽" : "生成结果"}</span>
              </div>
            `;
          }).join("");

          return `
            <section class="runtime-group">
              <div class="runtime-group-head">
                <div>
                  <strong>${escapeHtml(cleanTitle(title))}</strong><br>
                  <span>${escapeHtml(sub || "最近生成")}</span>
                </div>
                <button class="btn small" data-retry-group="${groupIndex}">重跑本组</button>
              </div>
              <div class="runtime-body">${views}</div>
            </section>
          `;
        }).join("");

        $$(".runtime-view").forEach((card) => {
          card.onclick = () => {
            const group = groups[Number(card.dataset.group || 0)];
            const variant = getVariants().find((item) => item.key === card.dataset.variant) || getCurrentVariant();
            const item = normalizeRuntimeItem((group.views || group.results || {})[variant.key]);
            openImageModal(`${variant.label} · ${variant.name}`, pickImage(item), runtimeDetailText(group, variant, item), {
              action: "retry_one",
              variant_key: variant.key,
              group
            });
          };
        });

        $$("[data-retry-group]").forEach((btn) => {
          btn.onclick = () => {
            const group = groups[Number(btn.dataset.retryGroup || 0)];
            sendRetryCommand("retry_all", null, group);
          };
        });
      }

      function renderPreview() {
        const isTemplate = appState.previewMode === "template";
        $("#templateViewer").style.display = isTemplate ? "grid" : "none";
        $("#runtimeGrid").style.display = isTemplate ? "none" : "grid";

        $$(".preview-switch button").forEach((btn) => {
          btn.classList.toggle("active", btn.dataset.previewMode === appState.previewMode);
        });

        if (isTemplate) renderTemplatePreview();
        else renderRuntime();
      }

      function renderAll() {
        ensureCurrentModeValid();
        renderSkin();
        renderDesignerSwitch();
        renderModes();
        renderPromptTabs();
        renderPromptEditor();
        renderPreview();
        $("#autoRetryToggle").checked = Boolean(appState.autoRetry);
        $("#footerText").textContent = `Hrio Design Prompt Editor · v${appState.pluginVersion || DEFAULT_PLUGIN_VERSION}`;
        setDirty(appState.dirty);
      }

      async function refreshRuntime(showTip = false) {
        $("#runtimePollState").textContent = "读取中...";
        for (const route of RUNTIME_GET_ROUTES) {
          try {
            const resp = await fetch(route, { cache: "no-store" });
            if (!resp.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${resp.status}`);
            appState.runtime = await resp.json();
            $("#runtimePollState").textContent = "已读取";
            renderPreview();
            maybeAutoRetryFailed();
            if (showTip) toast("已刷新，返回图会同步到当前预览");
            return true;
          } catch (error) {
            console.warn("runtime failed:", route, error);
          }
        }
        $("#runtimePollState").textContent = "读取失败";
        if (showTip) toast("运行结果接口暂不可用");
        return false;
      }

      async function clearRuntime() {
        for (const route of RUNTIME_CLEAR_ROUTES) {
          try {
            const resp = await fetch(route, { method: "POST" });
            if (resp.ok) break;
          } catch {}
        }
        appState.runtime = {};
        $("#runtimePollState").textContent = "已清空";
        renderPreview();
        toast("已清空前端预览缓存");
      }

      function statusText(status) {
        const s = String(status || "").toLowerCase();
        if (["success", "ok", "done"].includes(s)) return "成功";
        if (["failed", "fail", "error"].includes(s)) return "报错";
        if (["pending", "running"].includes(s)) return "生成中";
        if (["missing", "unknown"].includes(s)) return "暂无";
        return status || "未知";
      }

      function runtimeDetailText(group, variant, item) {
        return [
          `模板：${titleByModeKey(currentModeKey)}`,
          `方案：${variant.label} · ${variant.name}`,
          `状态：${statusText(item.status)}`,
          `模型：${group.model || "-"}`,
          `尺寸：${group.image_size || "-"}`,
          `比例：${group.aspect_ratio || "-"}`,
          `cache_key：${group.cache_key || "-"}`,
          "",
          item.error ? `报错：${item.error}` : "",
          pickImage(item) || ""
        ].filter(Boolean).join("\n");
      }

      function openImageModal(title, url, text, retryPayload = null) {
        $("#modalTitle").textContent = title || "结果详情";
        $("#modalImage").innerHTML = url
          ? `<img src="${escapeHtml(url)}" alt="${escapeHtml(title || "预览图")}" />`
          : `<div class="placeholder" style="border:0;">暂无图片</div>`;
        $("#modalText").textContent = text || "";
        appState.lastModalText = text || "";
        appState.lastModalImageUrl = url || "";
        appState.lastModalRetryPayload = retryPayload || currentVariantPayload("retry_one");
        $("#resultModal").classList.add("show");
      }

      function closeModal() {
        $("#resultModal").classList.remove("show");
      }

      function currentVariantPayload(action = "retry_one") {
        const variant = getCurrentVariant();
        return {
          action,
          variant_key: variant.key,
          variant_label: variant.label,
          variant_name: variant.name,
          template_mode: titleByModeKey(currentModeKey),
          mode_key: currentModeKey,
          mode_title: titleByModeKey(currentModeKey),
          designer_key: appState.designer,
          designer_display: appState.designer === "interior_design" ? "室内设计" : "平面设计",
          generate_scope: variant.key === "variant_a" ? "仅重新生成方案A" : variant.key === "variant_b" ? "仅重新生成方案B" : "仅重新生成方案C"
        };
      }

      function sendCommand(command) {
        const payload = {
          source: "banana_designer_prompt_editor",
          channel: COMMAND_CHANNEL,
          created_at: Date.now(),
          ...command
        };

        try {
          const bc = new BroadcastChannel(COMMAND_CHANNEL);
          bc.postMessage(payload);
          setTimeout(() => bc.close(), 500);
        } catch {}

        try {
          localStorage.setItem(COMMAND_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload));
        } catch {}

        try {
          if (window.opener && !window.opener.closed) window.opener.postMessage(payload, "*");
        } catch {}

        try {
          window.parent?.postMessage(payload, "*");
        } catch {}
      }

      function publishSync(reason = "edit", debouncedSave = false) {
        snapshotCurrentText();
        const payload = buildExportConfig({ skipSnapshot: true });
        const command = {
          type: "config_sync",
          action: "config_sync",
          reason,
          current_mode_key: currentModeKey,
          current_mode_title: titleByModeKey(currentModeKey),
          current_field: currentField,
          selected_variant_key: appState.selectedVariantKey,
          designer_key: appState.designer,
          designer_display: appState.designer === "interior_design" ? "室内设计" : "平面设计",
          mode_options: payload.mode_options,
          mode_meta: payload.mode_meta,
          preview_urls: payload.preview_urls,
          prompt_config: payload,
          config: payload,
          current_mode_config: getCurrentModeData()
        };

        localStorage.setItem(LIVE_CONFIG_KEY, JSON.stringify(command));
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload));
        sendCommand(command);

        if (debouncedSave) {
          clearTimeout(appState.saveTimer);
          appState.saveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            postConfig(payload, false);
          }, 900);
        }
      }

      function sendRetryCommand(action = "retry_one", variantKey = null, group = null) {
        const payload = variantKey
          ? { ...currentVariantPayload(action), variant_key: variantKey }
          : currentVariantPayload(action);

        if (action === "retry_all") {
          payload.generate_scope = "全部并发生成";
        }

        if (action === "retry_failed") {
          payload.generate_scope = "全部并发生成";
        }

        if (group) payload.group = group;

        sendCommand({
          type: "retry",
          ...payload,
          prompt_config: buildExportConfig({ skipSnapshot: false }),
          current_mode_config: getCurrentModeData(),
          full_prompt: composeFullPrompt()
        });

        toast(action === "retry_all" ? "已发送重跑全部命令" : "已发送重抽命令");
      }

      function maybeAutoRetryFailed() {
        if (!appState.autoRetry) return;

        const latest = getLatestRuntimeGroup();
        if (!latest) return;

        const failed = getVariants().filter((variant) => {
          const item = normalizeRuntimeItem((latest.views || latest.results || {})[variant.key]);
          const s = String(item.status || "").toLowerCase();
          return item.error || ["failed", "fail", "error"].includes(s);
        });

        if (!failed.length) return;

        const key = `${currentModeKey}:${failed.map((x) => x.key).join(",")}`;
        const now = Date.now();
        if (now - (appState.retryStamp[key] || 0) < 9000) return;
        appState.retryStamp[key] = now;

        sendCommand({
          type: "retry",
          action: "retry_failed",
          mode_key: currentModeKey,
          mode_title: titleByModeKey(currentModeKey),
          template_mode: titleByModeKey(currentModeKey),
          designer_key: appState.designer,
          failed_variants: failed.map((item) => item.key),
          generate_scope: "全部并发生成",
          prompt_config: buildExportConfig({ skipSnapshot: false })
        });
      }

      function buildExportConfig(options = {}) {
        if (!options.skipSnapshot) snapshotCurrentText();

        const modes = {};
        const preview_urls = {};

        Object.values(modeOptions).forEach((key) => {
          ensureModeExists(key, titleByModeKey(key));
          const data = configByMode[key] || createDefaultPrompt(modeDef(key));
          data.preview_urls = data.preview_urls || {};
          modes[key] = data;
          preview_urls[key] = {
            variant_a: data.preview_urls.variant_a || "",
            variant_b: data.preview_urls.variant_b || "",
            variant_c: data.preview_urls.variant_c || ""
          };
        });

        return {
          plugin_version: appState.pluginVersion || DEFAULT_PLUGIN_VERSION,
          version: Date.now(),
          description: "Hrio Design 设计师模板生成节点提示词配置。支持模板增删改复制、OSS 预览图、预览同步和真实重试。",
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          designer_type_options: DESIGNER_TYPE_OPTIONS,
          output_strategy_options: OUTPUT_STRATEGY_OPTIONS,
          creativity_options: CREATIVITY_OPTIONS,
          theme_skins: THEME_SKINS,
          mode_options: modeOptions,
          mode_meta: modeMeta,
          preview_urls,
          modes,
          global_negative_prompt: "不要真实文字，不要乱码字体，不要水印，不要二维码，不要价格，不要促销标签，不要购物按钮，不要低清晰度，不要明显 AI 扭曲，不要畸形结构，不要杂乱拼贴，不要廉价滤镜。"
        };
      }

      async function postConfig(payload, showToast = true) {
        for (const route of CONFIG_POST_ROUTES) {
          try {
            const resp = await fetch(route, {
              method: "POST",
              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
              body: JSON.stringify(payload)
            });
            if (!resp.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${resp.status}`);
            const json = await resp.json().catch(() => ({}));
            const saved = json.prompt_config || json.config || payload;
            localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(saved));
            setStatus(`配置状态：已保存到 ${route}`);
            setDirty(false);
            if (showToast) toast("配置已保存");
            return true;
          } catch (error) {
            console.warn("save failed:", route, error);
          }
        }
        setStatus("配置状态：后端不可写，已保存到本地缓存");
        return false;
      }

      async function saveConfig() {
        snapshotCurrentText();
        const payload = buildExportConfig({ skipSnapshot: true });
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload));
        publishSync("manual_save", false);

        const ok = await postConfig(payload, true);
        if (!ok) {
          downloadJson(payload, "banana_designer_prompts.json");
          toast("后端不可写，已导出 JSON；文件内包含 OSS 预览图 URL");
        }
      }

      function resetCurrentMode() {
        const oldPreview = getCurrentModeData().preview_urls || {};
        configByMode[currentModeKey] = deepClone(DEFAULT_PROMPTS[currentModeKey] || createDefaultPrompt(modeDef(currentModeKey)));
        configByMode[currentModeKey].preview_urls = oldPreview;
        renderAll();
        setDirty(true);
        publishSync("reset_mode", true);
        toast("当前模板已恢复默认，OSS 预览图已保留");
      }

      function clearLocalCache() {
        localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        localStorage.removeItem(UI_STATE_KEY);
        localStorage.removeItem(LIVE_CONFIG_KEY);
        setStatus("配置状态：本地缓存已清空");
        toast("已清空本地缓存");
      }

      function copyText(text, message) {
        if (navigator.clipboard?.writeText) {
          navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => toast(message || "已复制"), () => fallbackCopy(text, message));
        } else {
          fallbackCopy(text, message);
        }
      }

      function fallbackCopy(text, message) {
        const ta = document.createElement("textarea");
        ta.value = text;
        document.body.appendChild(ta);
        ta.select();
        document.execCommand("copy");
        ta.remove();
        toast(message || "已复制");
      }

      function downloadJson(data, filename) {
        downloadText(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2), filename, "application/json;charset=utf-8");
      }

      function downloadText(text, filename, type = "text/plain;charset=utf-8") {
        const blob = new Blob([text], { type });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement("a");
        a.href = url;
        a.download = filename;
        document.body.appendChild(a);
        a.click();
        a.remove();
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      }

      async function importConfigFile(file) {
        if (!file) return;
        try {
          const json = JSON.parse(await file.text());
          normalizeLoadedConfig(json);
          localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(json));
          ensureCurrentModeValid();
          renderAll();
          setDirty(true);
          publishSync("import_config", true);
          toast("配置已导入，模板与 OSS 预览图已更新");
        } catch {
          toast("导入失败：不是有效 JSON");
        }
      }

      function composeFullPrompt() {
        snapshotCurrentText();
        const data = getCurrentModeData();
        const title = titleByModeKey(currentModeKey);
        return [
          `【${title}】`,
          "",
          `【参考图说明】`,
          data.image_roles || "",
          "",
          `【全局任务】`,
          data.global_prompt || "",
          "",
          `【方案 A】`,
          data.variant_a_prompt || "",
          "",
          `【方案 B】`,
          data.variant_b_prompt || "",
          "",
          `【方案 C】`,
          data.variant_c_prompt || "",
          "",
          `【一致性要求】`,
          data.consistency_prompt || "",
          "",
          `【反向约束】`,
          data.negative_prompt || ""
        ].join("\n");
      }

      function currentModeJson() {
        snapshotCurrentText();
        return {
          title: titleByModeKey(currentModeKey),
          key: currentModeKey,
          meta: modeMeta[currentModeKey],
          ...getCurrentModeData()
        };
      }

      function openModeModal(type = "add") {
        appState.modeModalType = type;
        const def = modeDef(currentModeKey);

        $("#modeModalTitle").textContent = type === "edit" ? "编辑当前模板" : "新增模板";
        $("#modeTitleInput").value = type === "edit" ? titleByModeKey(currentModeKey) : "";
        $("#modeKeyInput").value = type === "edit" ? currentModeKey : "";
        $("#modeKeyInput").disabled = type === "edit";
        $("#modeDesignerInput").value = type === "edit" ? def.designer_type : appState.designer;
        $("#modeIconInput").value = type === "edit" ? def.icon : "新";
        $("#modeDescInput").value = type === "edit" ? def.desc : "自定义设计模板。";

        $("#modeModal").classList.add("show");
      }

      function closeModeModal() {
        $("#modeModal").classList.remove("show");
      }

      function slugify(value) {
        const raw = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        const ascii = raw
          .replace(/[^\w\s-]/g, "")
          .replace(/\s+/g, "_")
          .replace(/-+/g, "_")
          .replace(/_+/g, "_")
          .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
        return ascii || `custom_mode_${Date.now()}`;
      }

      function saveModeFromModal() {
        const type = appState.modeModalType;
        const title = $("#modeTitleInput").value.trim();
        const key = type === "edit" ? currentModeKey : slugify($("#modeKeyInput").value || title);
        const designerType = $("#modeDesignerInput").value;
        const icon = $("#modeIconInput").value.trim() || title.slice(0, 1) || "新";
        const desc = $("#modeDescInput").value.trim() || "自定义设计模板。";

        if (!title) {
          toast("请填写模板名称");
          return;
        }

        if (type !== "edit" && configByMode[key]) {
          toast("模板 Key 已存在，请换一个");
          return;
        }

        if (type === "edit") {
          Object.keys(modeOptions).forEach((name) => {
            if (modeOptions[name] === currentModeKey && name !== title) delete modeOptions[name];
          });
        }

        modeOptions[title] = key;
        modeMeta[key] = {
          ...(modeMeta[key] || {}),
          title,
          designer_type: designerType,
          icon,
          desc,
          variants: modeMeta[key]?.variants || createFallbackVariants(key)
        };

        if (!configByMode[key]) {
          configByMode[key] = createDefaultPrompt(modeMeta[key]);
        }

        configByMode[key].designer_type = designerType;
        configByMode[key].preview_urls = configByMode[key].preview_urls || {};

        currentModeKey = key;
        appState.designer = designerType;
        currentField = "image_roles";
        appState.selectedVariantKey = getVariants(key)[0]?.key || "variant_a";

        closeModeModal();
        renderAll();
        setDirty(true);
        publishSync(type === "edit" ? "edit_mode" : "add_mode", true);
        toast(type === "edit" ? "模板已更新" : "模板已新增，可上传预览图");
      }

      function duplicateCurrentMode() {
        snapshotCurrentText();

        const oldTitle = titleByModeKey(currentModeKey);
        const newTitle = `${oldTitle} 副本`;
        const newKey = `${currentModeKey}_copy_${Date.now()}`;

        modeOptions[newTitle] = newKey;
        modeMeta[newKey] = {
          ...deepClone(modeMeta[currentModeKey] || modeDef(currentModeKey)),
          title: newTitle
        };
        configByMode[newKey] = deepClone(getCurrentModeData());

        currentModeKey = newKey;
        currentField = "image_roles";
        appState.selectedVariantKey = getVariants(newKey)[0]?.key || "variant_a";

        renderAll();
        setDirty(true);
        publishSync("duplicate_mode", true);
        toast("已复制当前模板，提示词和 OSS 预览图已一起复制");
      }

      function deleteCurrentMode() {
        const sameDesignerKeys = Object.values(modeOptions).filter((key) => modeDef(key).designer_type === appState.designer);
        if (sameDesignerKeys.length <= 1) {
          toast("当前设计方向至少保留一个模板");
          return;
        }

        const title = titleByModeKey(currentModeKey);
        if (!confirm(`确定删除「${title}」吗？`)) return;

        Object.keys(modeOptions).forEach((name) => {
          if (modeOptions[name] === currentModeKey) delete modeOptions[name];
        });
        delete modeMeta[currentModeKey];
        delete configByMode[currentModeKey];

        currentModeKey = Object.values(modeOptions).find((key) => modeDef(key).designer_type === appState.designer) || Object.values(modeOptions)[0];
        currentField = "image_roles";
        appState.selectedVariantKey = getVariants(currentModeKey)[0]?.key || "variant_a";

        renderAll();
        setDirty(true);
        publishSync("delete_mode", true);
        toast("模板已删除");
      }

      function makeApiUrl(base, route) {
        const b = String(base || "").replace(/\/+$/, "");
        const r = String(route || "");
        if (!b) return r;
        if (r.startsWith("/api/") && b.endsWith("/api")) return b + r.slice(4);
        return b + r;
      }

      async function fetchWithTimeout(url, options = {}, timeoutMs = 30000) {
        const controller = new AbortController();
        const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
        try {
          return await fetch(url, { ...options, signal: controller.signal });
        } finally {
          clearTimeout(timer);
        }
      }

      function extractNested(obj, keys) {
        if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object") return "";
        for (const key of keys) if (obj[key]) return obj[key];
        for (const item of [obj.data, obj.result, obj.file, obj.upload, obj.oss, obj.payload]) {
          if (!item || typeof item !== "object") continue;
          for (const key of keys) if (item[key]) return item[key];
        }
        return "";
      }

      function extractPublicUrl(data) {
        return extractNested(data, ["url", "oss_url", "ossUrl", "public_url", "publicUrl", "file_url", "fileUrl", "download_url", "downloadUrl", "preview_url", "previewUrl", "cdn_url", "cdnUrl"]);
      }

      function extractUploadUrl(data) {
        return extractNested(data, ["upload_url", "uploadUrl", "presigned_url", "presignedUrl", "put_url", "putUrl", "signed_url", "signedUrl"]);
      }

      function extractPostUrl(data) {
        return extractNested(data, ["post_url", "postUrl", "host", "url_for_post", "urlForPost"]);
      }

      function extractFields(data) {
        return data?.fields || data?.form || data?.formData || data?.data?.fields || data?.data?.form || null;
      }

      async function uploadBySimpleRoute(file) {
        for (const base of API_BASE_CANDIDATES) {
          for (const route of SIMPLE_UPLOAD_ROUTES) {
            const url = makeApiUrl(base, route);
            try {
              const form = new FormData();
              form.append("file", file);
              form.append("mode_key", currentModeKey);
              form.append("mode_title", titleByModeKey(currentModeKey));
              form.append("variant_key", appState.selectedVariantKey);
              form.append("folder", `assets/plu/custom/${currentModeKey}`);

              const resp = await fetchWithTimeout(url, { method: "POST", body: form }, 60000);
              if (!resp.ok) continue;

              const data = await resp.json().catch(() => ({}));
              const publicUrl = extractPublicUrl(data);
              if (publicUrl) return publicUrl;
            } catch (error) {
              console.warn("simple upload failed:", url, error);
            }
          }
        }
        return "";
      }

      async function uploadByPresign(file) {
        for (const base of API_BASE_CANDIDATES) {
          for (const route of PRESIGN_ROUTES) {
            const url = makeApiUrl(base, route);
            try {
              const ext = file.name.includes(".") ? file.name.split(".").pop() : "png";
              const filename = `${currentModeKey}_${appState.selectedVariantKey}_${Date.now()}.${ext}`;
              const objectKey = `assets/plu/custom/${currentModeKey}/${filename}`;

              const resp = await fetchWithTimeout(url, {
                method: "POST",
                headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                  filename,
                  file_name: filename,
                  key: objectKey,
                  object_key: objectKey,
                  path: objectKey,
                  content_type: file.type || "image/png",
                  mime_type: file.type || "image/png",
                  size: file.size,
                  mode_key: currentModeKey,
                  mode_title: titleByModeKey(currentModeKey),
                  variant_key: appState.selectedVariantKey,
                  folder: `assets/plu/custom/${currentModeKey}`
                })
              }, 30000);

              if (!resp.ok) continue;

              const data = await resp.json().catch(() => ({}));
              const uploadUrl = extractUploadUrl(data);
              const postUrl = extractPostUrl(data);
              const fields = extractFields(data);
              const publicUrl = extractPublicUrl(data);

              if (uploadUrl) {
                const putResp = await fetchWithTimeout(uploadUrl, {
                  method: "PUT",
                  headers: { "Content-Type": file.type || "application/octet-stream" },
                  body: file
                }, 90000);
                if (!putResp.ok) throw new Error(`PUT OSS failed HTTP ${putResp.status}`);
                return publicUrl || String(uploadUrl).split("?")[0];
              }

              if (postUrl && fields && typeof fields === "object") {
                const form = new FormData();
                Object.entries(fields).forEach(([k, v]) => form.append(k, v));
                form.append("file", file);

                const postResp = await fetchWithTimeout(postUrl, { method: "POST", body: form }, 90000);
                if (!postResp.ok) throw new Error(`POST OSS failed HTTP ${postResp.status}`);
                return publicUrl || `${String(postUrl).replace(/\/+$/, "")}/${fields.key || objectKey}`;
              }

              if (publicUrl) return publicUrl;
            } catch (error) {
              console.warn("presign upload failed:", url, error);
            }
          }
        }
        return "";
      }

      async function uploadPreviewFile(file) {
        const simple = await uploadBySimpleRoute(file);
        if (simple) return simple;
        const presign = await uploadByPresign(file);
        if (presign) return presign;
        throw new Error("上传失败：没有可用上传接口，或接口没有返回 URL");
      }

      async function handlePreviewUpload(file) {
        if (!file) return;
        try {
          toast("正在上传预览图到 OSS...");
          const url = await uploadPreviewFile(file);
          const data = getCurrentModeData();
          data.preview_urls = data.preview_urls || {};
          data.preview_urls[appState.selectedVariantKey] = url;
          setDirty(true);
          renderPreview();
          publishSync("upload_preview", true);
          toast("预览图已上传并写入配置");
        } catch (error) {
          console.error(error);
          toast(error.message || "上传失败");
        }
      }

      function bindEvents() {
        $("#promptText").addEventListener("input", () => {
          getCurrentModeData()[currentField] = $("#promptText").value;
          $("#charCount").textContent = `${$("#promptText").value.length} 字符`;
          setDirty(true);
          cacheConfig();
          publishSync("input", true);
        });

        $$(".designer-btn").forEach((btn) => {
          btn.onclick = () => {
            snapshotCurrentText();
            appState.designer = btn.dataset.designer;
            restoreBuiltInModes();
            ensureCurrentModeValid();
            saveUiState();
            renderAll();
            publishSync("switch_designer", true);
          };
        });

        $$(".skin-toggle button").forEach((btn) => {
          btn.onclick = () => {
            appState.skinKey = btn.dataset.skin;
            saveUiState();
            renderSkin();
          };
        });

        $$(".preview-switch button").forEach((btn) => {
          btn.onclick = () => {
            appState.previewMode = btn.dataset.previewMode;
            saveUiState();
            renderPreview();
            if (appState.previewMode !== "template") refreshRuntime();
          };
        });

        $("#saveBtn").onclick = saveConfig;
        $("#exportBtn").onclick = () => downloadJson(buildExportConfig(), "banana_designer_prompts.json");
        $("#importBtn").onclick = () => $("#importFile").click();
        $("#importFile").onchange = (event) => {
          importConfigFile(event.target.files?.[0]);
          event.target.value = "";
        };

        $("#resetModeBtn").onclick = resetCurrentMode;
        $("#clearLocalBtn").onclick = clearLocalCache;
        $("#addModeBtn").onclick = () => openModeModal("add");
        $("#editModeBtn").onclick = () => openModeModal("edit");
        $("#copyModeBtn").onclick = duplicateCurrentMode;
        $("#deleteModeBtn").onclick = deleteCurrentMode;

        $("#modeModalCloseBtn").onclick = closeModeModal;
        $("#modeModal").onclick = (event) => {
          if (event.target.id === "modeModal") closeModeModal();
        };
        $("#modeSaveBtn").onclick = saveModeFromModal;
        $("#duplicateDefaultPromptBtn").onclick = () => {
          $("#modeDescInput").value = modeDef(currentModeKey).desc;
          toast("已套用当前模板描述；保存后会复制当前提示词");
        };

        $("#copyCurrentBtn").onclick = () => copyText(getCurrentModeData()[currentField] || "", "已复制当前字段");
        $("#copyFullPromptBtn").onclick = () => copyText(composeFullPrompt(), "已复制整组提示词");
        $("#copyCurrentJsonBtn").onclick = () => copyText(JSON.stringify(currentModeJson(), null, 2), "已复制当前模板 JSON");
        $("#exportPromptTxtBtn").onclick = () => downloadText(composeFullPrompt(), `${currentModeKey}_prompt.txt`);

        $("#refreshRuntimeBtn").onclick = () => refreshRuntime(true);
        $("#retryCurrentBtn").onclick = () => sendRetryCommand("retry_one");
        $("#retryAllBtn").onclick = () => sendRetryCommand("retry_all");
        $("#retryFailedBtn").onclick = () => sendRetryCommand("retry_failed");
        $("#clearRuntimeBtn").onclick = clearRuntime;

        $("#autoRetryToggle").onchange = (event) => {
          appState.autoRetry = Boolean(event.target.checked);
          saveUiState();
          toast(appState.autoRetry ? "已开启报错自动重抽" : "已关闭报错自动重抽");
        };

        $("#previewStage").onclick = (event) => {
          if (event.target.closest("button")) return;
          const variant = getCurrentVariant();
          const src = previewSourceForVariant(variant);
          openImageModal(`${variant.label} · ${variant.name}`, src.url, [
            `模板：${titleByModeKey(currentModeKey)}`,
            `方案：${variant.label} · ${variant.name}`,
            `来源：${src.source}`,
            "",
            src.url
          ].join("\n"), currentVariantPayload("retry_one"));
        };

        $("#uploadPreviewBtn").onclick = (event) => {
          event.stopPropagation();
          $("#previewUploadFile").click();
        };

        $("#previewUploadFile").onchange = (event) => {
          handlePreviewUpload(event.target.files?.[0]);
          event.target.value = "";
        };

        $("#modalCloseBtn").onclick = closeModal;
        $("#resultModal").onclick = (event) => {
          if (event.target.id === "resultModal") closeModal();
        };
        $("#modalCopyBtn").onclick = () => copyText(appState.lastModalText || $("#modalText").textContent || "", "已复制详情");
        $("#modalCopyUrlBtn").onclick = () => copyText(appState.lastModalImageUrl || "", "已复制图片 URL");
        $("#modalCopyPromptBtn").onclick = () => copyText(composeFullPrompt(), "已复制当前模板提示词");
        $("#modalRetryBtn").onclick = () => {
          const payload = appState.lastModalRetryPayload || currentVariantPayload("retry_one");
          sendCommand({
            type: "retry",
            ...payload,
            prompt_config: buildExportConfig({ skipSnapshot: false }),
            current_mode_config: getCurrentModeData()
          });
          toast("已发送当前图重抽命令");
        };

        document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
          if (event.key === "Escape") {
            closeModal();
            closeModeModal();
          }
          if ((event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) && event.key.toLowerCase() === "s") {
            event.preventDefault();
            saveConfig();
          }
        });
      }

      async function init() {
        bindEvents();
        await loadConfig();
        await refreshRuntime(false);
        setInterval(() => refreshRuntime(false), 5000);
      }

      init();
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
